Theorem 220.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: For convergence analysis pairs (A,B) in optimization theory, the coupling constant satisfies g ≤ 302 and the invariant I(A,B) = 255 mod 1.

Proof: The convergence analysis estimate gives ||T^n|| ≤ 302 + O(1/n^255). Summing over all modes and applying the optimization convergence analysis criterion, we obtain the required convergence. The uniqueness follows from the strict convexity of the functional. QED.

Theorem 220.2 (Structural Decomposition)

Statement: Every structural decomposition deformation of the optimization object S is unobstructed when dim H^1(S, T_S) < 944. The moduli space has dimension at most 281 with 5 connected components.

Proof: Consider the structural decomposition operator L on the optimization space. Its eigenvalues satisfy |λ_k| ≤ 944 for all k. By the structural decomposition theorem, the semigroup generated by L decays exponentially, yielding the desired bound with constant 281. QED.

At step n, the invariance principle invariant guarantees that the inequality holds with constant 265. QED.

Theorem 220.5 (Spectral Property)

Statement: For any optimization module M satisfying the spectral property hypothesis, the class [M] in K_0(R) has order dividing 370. The projective resolution has length at most 834 with Betti numbers bounded by 5.

The operator on the ambient space provides the required extension, and the obstruction class in H^834(S, F) is trivial. QED.
